under a disciplined and risk-controlled approach.

segment. Inventory levels are closely monitored to mitigate product   管理該業務分部。存貨水平受到密切監

credit assessment procedures. The Group does not undertake            予則依據內部信貸評估程序。本集團不
speculative trading activities and does not maintain excessive        從事投機性交易活動，亦不持有過量庫

conditions and supplier stability and will adopt a measured           商穩定性，並在擴展該業務時採取審慎
approach in scaling this segment to ensure that the Group maintains   步伐，以維持本集團之保守風險狀況。
a conservative risk profile.

Looking forward, the Group will continue to evaluate the              展望未來，本集團將根據市場環境及營
performance of the mobile phone distribution business in light of     運效率持續評估手機分銷業務之表現。
prevailing market conditions and operational efficiency. Any          任何進一步擴展均將配合審慎之營運資
expansion of this segment will be aligned with prudent working        金管理及風險控制原則。董事會將繼續
capital management and risk control considerations. The Board         致力尋求符合本集團營運能力及長遠股
remains committed to pursuing diversification opportunities that      東利益之多元化發展機遇。
are consistent with the Group’s operational capabilities and long-

THE BOARD’S RESPONSE TO THE AUDITOR’S OPINION                          董事會對核數師意見之回應及解決核數
TO ADDRESS THE AUDITOR’S CONCERN                                       師關注事項之措施

The Board acknowledges the Qualified Opinion issued by the             董事會認可獨立核數師發出的保留意見，
independent auditor and places great importance on strengthening       並高度重視加強本集團的財務報告及內
the Group’s financial reporting and internal control framework. In     部監控框架。針對核數師的關注事項，董
response to the auditor’s concerns, the Board wishes to inform         事會謹此告知股東及潛在投資者以下行
shareholders and potential investors of the following actions and      動及計劃：
plans:

(a) Enhancement of Accounting System and Internal Controls             (a) 優化會計系統及內部監控

   The Board recognises that the Qualified Opinion arose                董事會確認，保留意見主要源於 CS
   primarily from the historical inadequacy of accounting books,        集團若干應收款項、應付款項及應計
   records and supporting documentation in relation to certain          項目的會計賬簿、記錄及證明文件存
   receivables, payables and accruals of the CS Group. To address       在歷史性不足。為解決此問題，本公
   this issue, the Company has commenced and will continue to           司已開始並將繼續實施加強其會計
   implement measures to enhance its accounting system and              系統及內部監控環境的措施，包括但
   internal control environment, including but not limited to:          不限於：

    - upgrading and standardising the accounting and record-             - 在全集團範圍內升級並標準化會
      keeping procedures across the Group;                                 計及記錄保存程序；

    - strengthening internal documentation requirements for              - 加強應收款項、應付款項及應計項
      receivables, payables and accruals, including proper                 目的內部文件要求，包括妥善的協
      agreements, confirmations and reconciliation procedures;             議、確認書及對賬程序；以及
      and

    - enhancing oversight by senior management over the                  - 加強高級管理層對會計職能的監
      accounting function.                                                 督。

   The Board believes that these measures will enable the Group         董事會相信，該等措施將使本集團日
   to maintain more comprehensive and reliable accounting               後能維持更全面及可靠的會計記錄，
   records going forward and facilitate the independent auditor in      並有助獨立核數師在未來的審核中
   obtaining sufficient appropriate audit evidence in future audits,    取得充足且適當的審核憑證，以期解
   with the objective of resolving the qualification.                   決保留意見問題。

Board Diversity Policy                                                   董事會多元化政策

The Company adopted a board diversity policy (the “Board Diversity       本公司採納董事會多元化政策（「董事
Policy”), which was amended on 12 February 2019. The Company             會多元化政策」），該政策於二零一九
recognised the benefits of having a diverse Board to enhance the         年二月十二日修訂。本公司認同設立多
quality of its performance. The Board Diversity Policy aimed to set      元化董事會以提高其績效質量的好處。
out the approach to achieve diversity on the Board. In designing the     董事會多元化政策旨在闡明實現董事
Board’s composition, Board diversity has been considered from a          會多元化的方法。於設計董事會組成時
number of measurable aspects including but not limited to gender,        已從多個可衡量的方面考慮董事會多
age, cultural and educational background, professional experience,       元化，包括但不限於性別、年齡、文化
skills, knowledge and length of services, all of which the Company       及教育背景、專業經驗、技能、知識及
considers to be important to enhance the quality of its performance.     服務時間長短，本公司認為該等對於提
All Board appointments will be based on meritocracy, and candidates      高其績效質量均為重要。所有董事會的
will be considered against objective criteria, having due regards for    委任均以用人唯才為原則，將以客觀標

The Group strives to maintain the integrity of its business, results of     本集團嚴格遵守有關業務的內部監控

number of key control areas such as tendering, purchase and                 理、財務管理及安全及環境合規管理等
procurement management, financial management and safety and                 多個主要監控範疇，以期確保本集團遵
environment compliance management with a view to ensuring                   守適用法律、規例及法規。
compliance by the Group with applicable laws, rules and regulations.

overseeing and monitoring the key measures adopted by the Group             督本集團在風險管理及內部監控系統

the business operations of the Company and assess the effectiveness         措施，並定期評估其有效性。於報告年
regularly. Review on the key measures adopted by the Group under            度已對本集團在風險管理及內部監控

Group’s business operations has been conducted for the Reporting            主要措施進行審閱。
Year.

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                        股息政策

The Company has adopted a dividend policy on 12 February 2019,         本公司已於二零一九年二月十二日採
pursuant to which, the Board considers the following factors when      納股息政策，據此，董事會在考慮派付
considering the payment of dividends:                                  股息時會考慮以下因素：

 i.    general financial conditions of the Group;                      i.   本集團的一般財務狀況；

 ii.   retained earnings and distributable reserves of the Company     ii. 本公司及本集團各成員公司的保
       and each of the members of the Group;                               留盈利及可分配儲備；

 iii. actual and future operations and liquidity positions of the      iii. 本集團的實際及未來營運以及流
      Group;                                                                動資金狀況；

 iv. future cash requirements and availability of the Group,           iv. 本集團未來現金需要及可動用程
     including its expected working capital requirements, capital          度，包括其預期營運資金需要、資
     expenditure requirements and future expansion plans;                  金開支需要及未來擴展計劃；

 v.    any restrictions on payments of dividends that may be           v. 本集團貸款人可能對派付股息施

2. STATEMENT OF COMPLIANCE AND BASIS OF                              2. 合規聲明及編製依據
   PREPARATION

  These consolidated financial statements have been prepared          這些合併財務報表是依照國際財務報
  in accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards and the                告準則和香港公司條例第 622 章的揭
  disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies                  露要求編製的。這些合併財務報表也符
  Ordinance Cap. 622. These consolidated financial statements         合香港聯合交易所有限公司創業板證
  also comply with the applicable disclosure provisions of the        券上市規則的適用揭露規定。
  Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on GEM of The
  Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited.

  These consolidated financial statements have been prepared          除某些以公允價值計量的金融資產外，
  on a historical cost basis, except for certain financial assets     這些合併財務報表均以歷史成本編製。
  measured at fair value.

  The preparation of these consolidated financial statements in       依照國際財務報告準則（IFRS）編製這
  conformity with IFRS Accounting Standards requires                  些合併財務報表，需要管理階層做出判
  management to make judgements, estimates and assumptions            斷、估計和假設，這些判斷、估計和假
  that affect the application of policies and reported amounts of     設會影響政策的運用以及資產、負債、
  assets, liabilities, income and expenses. The estimates and         收入和費用的報告金額。這些估計和相
  associated assumptions are based on historical experience and       關假設是基於歷史經驗和在當時情況
  various factors that are believed to be reasonable under the        下被認為合理的各種因素，其結果構成
  circumstances, the results of which form the basis of making        了對資產和負債帳面價值做出判斷的
  the judgements about carrying values of assets and liabilities      基礎，而這些價值無法從其他來源直接
  that are not readily apparent from other sources. Actual            獲得。實際結果可能與這些估計值有差
  results may differ from these estimates.                            異。

  The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an         會計估計及其基本假設會持續進行審
  ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are                查。如果會計估計的修訂僅影響修訂期
  recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised if the    間，則在該修訂期間確認修訂；如果修
  revision affects only that period, or in the period of the          訂同時影響當期和未來期間，則在修訂
  revision and future periods if the revision affects both current    期間和未來期間確認修訂。
  and future periods.

  Judgements made by management in the application of IFRS            管理階層在應用國際財務報告準則時
  Accounting Standards that have significant effect on the            所做的對合併財務報表有重大影響的
  consolidated financial statements and major sources of              判斷以及估計不確定性的主要來源，在

  Subsidiaries                                                          附屬公司

  Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over    附屬公司是指集團對其擁有控制權的
  which the Group has control. The Group controls an entity             所有實體（包括結構化實體）。集團控
  where the Group is exposed to, or has rights to, variable             制的實體是指集團因其參與該實體而
  returns from its involvement with the entity and has the ability      享有或可能享有可變收益，且集團能夠
  to affect those returns through its power to direct the activities    透過其對該實體活動的指導權來影響
  of the entity. Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date      這些收益的實體。自控制權轉移至集團
  on which control is transferred to the Group. They are                之日起，附屬公司即被完全合併。自控

Foreign currency translation                                       外幣折算

In preparing the financial statements of each individual group     在編製各集團實體的財務報表時，以該
entity, transactions in currencies other than the functional       實體功能貨幣以外的貨幣（外幣）進行
currency of that entity (foreign currencies) are recorded in the   的交易，均以相應的功能貨幣（即該實
respective functional currency (i.e. the currency of the primary   體主要經營所在經濟環境的貨幣）按交
economic environment in which the entity operates) at the          易日的匯率入帳。在報告期末，以外幣
rates of exchanges prevailing at the dates of the transactions.    計價的貨幣性項目以期末匯率折算。以
At the end of the reporting period, monetary items                 外幣計價並以公允價值計量的非貨幣
denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at the rates    性項目，以確定公允價值日的匯率折
prevailing at that date. Non-monetary items carried at fair        算。以外幣計價並以歷史成本計量的非
value that are denominated in foreign currencies are               貨幣性項目不進行折算。
retranslated at the rates prevailing at the date when the fair
value was determined. Non-monetary items that are measured
in terms of historical cost in a foreign currency are not
retranslated.

Exchange differences arising on the settlement of monetary         因結算貨幣性項目及貨幣性項目重估
items, and on the retranslation of monetary items, are             而產生的匯兌差額，應在其發生期間計
recognised in profit or loss in the period in which they arise.    入損益。因重估以公允價值計量的非貨
Exchange differences arising on the retranslation of               幣性項目而產生的匯兌差額，應計入當

or loss for the period.

each reporting period. Income and expenses items are                匯兌差額，則計入其他綜合收益，並在
translated at the average exchange rates for the year. Exchange     權益中計入「匯兌準備」。

   (“FVTPL”).                                                       損益的金融資產（「FVTPL」）。

managing the financial assets and the contractual terms of the    務模式和現金流的合約條款。
cash flows.

                                                                                    。

has made an irrevocable election at the time of initial           已作出不可撤銷的選擇，即在 OCI 中
recognition to present subsequent changes in fair value in        列示後續公允價值變動。
OCI.

                                                                  分類。

Provisions                                                           撥備

Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present legal         當集團因過去的事件而負有現行法律
or constructive obligation as a result of past events; it is         或推定義務，且很可能需要流出資源以
probable that an outflow of resources will be required to settle     清償該義務，並且金額已得到可靠估計
the obligation and the amount has been reliably estimated.           時，應確認撥備。未來經營虧損不計提
Provisions are not recognised for future operating losses.           撥備。

Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                 如果存在若干類似債務，結算時需要資
likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement is         金流出的可能性應考慮該類債務的整
determined by considering the class of obligations as a whole.       體情況來確定。即使同一類債務中任何
A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow       單一項目的資金流出可能性很小，也應
with respect to any one item included in the same class of           確認準備金。
obligations may be small.

Provisions are measured at the present value of management's         準備金按管理階層對報告期末清償當
best estimate of the expenditures required to settle the present     前債務所需支出的最佳估計值的現值
obligation at the end of the reporting period. The discount rate     計量。用於確定現值的折現率為稅前利
used to determine the present value is a pretax rate that reflects   率，反映了當前市場對貨幣時間價值和
current market assessments of the time value of money and            該債務特定風險的評估。因時間推移而
the risks specific to the obligation. The increase in the            增加的準備金確認為利息費用。
provision due to passage of time is recognised as interest
expense.

Construction contracts                                                建築合約

A contract with a customer is classified by the Group as a            當與客戶簽訂的合約涉及對客戶控制下
construction contract when the contract relates to work on an         的資產進行施工，並且集團的施工活動
asset under the control of the customer and therefore the Group’s     創造或增強了客戶控制下的資產時，集
construction activities create or enhance an asset under the          團會將該合約歸類為施工合約。
customer’s control.

When the outcome of a construction contract can be reasonably         當建築合約的成果能夠合理計量時，合
measured, revenue from the contract is recognised over time           約收入將採用成本法分期確認，即根據
using the cost-to-cost method, i.e. based on the proportion of the    實際發生的成本佔總成本的比例確認收
actual costs incurred relative to the estimated total costs. In the   入。在合約初期，集團通常無法計量其
early stage of a contract, the Group is generally not able to         履約義務的成果，但預期能夠收回已發
measure the outcome of its performance obligations but expects        生的合約成本。在集團能夠可靠地計量
to recover the contract costs incurred. Revenue is recognised to      履約義務的成果之前，收入將根據已發
the extent of those costs until such time that the Group can          生的成本確認。
reliably measure the outcome of the performance obligation.

If at any time the costs to complete the contract are estimated to    如果任何時候完成合約的成本預計超過
exceed the remaining amount of the consideration under the            合約項下剩餘的對價金額，則應根據虧
contract, then a provision is recognised in accordance with an        損合約確認準備金。當集團擁有的合約
onerous contract. An onerous contract exists when the Group has       中，履行合約義務的不可避免的成本超
a contract under which the unavoidable costs of meeting the           過預期從該合約獲得的經濟利益時，即
obligations under the contract exceed the economic benefits           構成虧損合約。虧損合約的準備金以終
expected to be received from the contract. Provisions for onerous     止合約的預期成本與繼續履行合約的淨
contracts are measured at the present value of the lower of the       成本兩者中較低者的現值計量。
expected cost of terminating the contract and the net cost of
continuing with the contract.

Revenue from provision of structural reinforced and concrete          一般建築和土木工程中的結構鋼筋混凝
works in general building and civil engineering works and other       土工程及其他服務的收入會隨著時間的
services is recognised over time.                                     推移而確認。

Revenue from provision of labour supply in construction industry      建築業勞動力供應收入是分期確認的。
is recognised over time.

30. RESERVES                                                               30. 儲備

(a) Group                                                                  (a) 本集團

    Details of the movements on the Group’s reserves are set out in        本集團的儲備變動詳情載於綜合權益變
    the consolidated statement of changes in equity.                       動表。

    The following describes the nature and purpose of each reserve         所有者權益中各儲備的性質及目的說明
    within owners’ equity:                                                 如下：

    Share premium                                                          股份溢價
    The share premium represents the excess of the proceeds received       股份溢價指所收到的收益超過本公司已
    over the nominal value of the Company’s shares issued.                 發行股份的面值的部分。

    Merger reserve                                                         合併儲備
    Merger reserve represents the difference between the cost of           合併儲備指根據重組進行收購的成本與
    acquisition pursuant to the reorganisation and the total value of      所收購實體的股本總值之間的差額。
    share capital of the entities acquired.

    Translation reserve                                                    匯兌儲備
    The translation reserve comprises all foreign exchange                 匯兌儲備包括所有因換算海外業務財務
    differences arising from the translation of the financial statements   報表產生的外匯差額。該儲備根據綜合
    of foreign operations. The reserve is dealt with in accordance with    財務報表附註 3 所載的會計政策處置。
    the accounting policies set out in Note 3 to the consolidated
    financial statements.

(c) Credit risk                                                       (c) 信貸風險

    Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s counterparties      信貸風險指本集團的交易對手方未
    default on their contractual obligations resulting in financial     能履行其合約責任而導致本集團出
    losses to the Group. As at 31 December 2025, the Group’s            現財務虧損的風險。於二零二五年
    maximum exposure to credit risk which will cause a                  十二月三十一日，本集團面臨的最
    financial loss to the Group due to failure to discharge an          大信貸風險為來自綜合財務狀況表
    obligation by the counterparties is arising from the carrying       中所列各項財務資產及合約資產的
    amount of respective financial assets and contract assets as        賬面值，該等風險將導致本集團因
    stated in the consolidated statement of financial position.         交易對手方未能履行義務而遭受財
                                                                        務虧損。
    The credit risk of the Group mainly arises from cash and            本集團的信貸風險主要來自於現金
    cash equivalents, trade and other receivables and contract          及現金等價物、貿易及其他應收款
    assets. The carrying amounts of these balances represent our        項以及合約資產。該等結餘的賬面
    Group’s maximum exposure to credit risk in relation to              值代表本集團有關財務資產及合約
    financial assets and contract assets.                               資產的最大信貸風險。

    The objective of the Group’s measures to manage credit risk         本集團管理信貸風險措施的目的為
    is to control potential exposure to recoverability problem.         控制潛在的可收回性問題。本集團
    Most of the Group’s cash and cash equivalents are held in           的大部分現金及現金等價物均存放
    major reputable financial institutions, which management            於主要的信譽良好的金融機構，管
    believes are of high credit quality.                                理 層 認 為 該等 機 構具 有高 信 用 質
                                                                        素。

38.    FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS                                               38. 公允價值計量

       Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset         公允價值是指在計量日，市場參與
       or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between       者在有序交易中出售資產所能收
       market participants at the measurement date. The following              到的價格，或轉移負債所能支付的
       disclosures of fair value measurements use a fair value                 價格。以下公允價值計量揭露採用
       hierarchy that categorizes into three levels the inputs to              公允價值層級結構，將用於計量公
       valuation techniques used to measure fair value:                        允價值的估價技術的輸入分為三
                                                                               個層級：

         Level 1 inputs:       quoted prices (unadjusted) in active            一級輸入：在計量日，集團可在活
                               markets for identical assets or liabilities     躍市場上獲得的相同資產或負債
                               that the Group can access at the                的報價（未經調整）。
                               measurement date.

         Level 2 inputs:       inputs other than quoted prices included        二級輸入：除一級輸入之外，資產
                               within level 1 that are observable for the      或負債可直接或間接觀察到的投
                               asset or liability, either directly or          入。
                               indirectly.

         Level 3 inputs:       unobservable inputs for the asset or            三級輸入：資產或負債的不可觀察
                               liability.                                      輸入。


       The Group's policy is to recognise transfers into and transfers         集團的政策是，對於三個層級中
       out of any of the three levels as of the date of the event or           的任何層級的轉入和轉出，均以
       change in circumstances that caused the transfer.                       導致轉入或轉出事件或情況變化
                                                                               的日期為準。

39.    CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                39. 資本管理

       The Group's objective of managing capital is to safeguard the       集團的資本管理目標是保障集團
       Group's ability to continue as a going concern in order to          持續經營能力，進而為股東創造回
       provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other             報，為其他利害關係人帶來利益，
       stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to        並維持最佳資本結構以降低資本
       reduce cost of capital. The Group's overall strategy remains        成本。集團的整體策略與上年保持
       unchanged from prior year.                                          不變。

       In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group     為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可
       may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders,            調整向股東支付的股利金額、向股
       return capital to shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets     東返還資本、發行新股或出售資產
       to reduce debts. The capital structure of the Group consists of     以減少債務。本集團的資本結構包
       debts, which includes the bank borrowings (Note 26), other          括債務（包括銀行借款（附註 26）、
       borrowings (Note 26), lease liabilities (Note 27), cash and         其他借款（附註 26）
                                                                                     、租賃負債（附
       cash equivalents (Note 22) and total equity, comprising share       註 27）、現金及現金等價物（附註
       capital (Note 29) and reserves (Note 30). The Group's risk          22））及權益總額（包括股本（附
       management reviews the capital structure on a semiannual            註 29）及儲備金（附註 30））。本
       basis. As part of this review, management considers the cost        集團的風險管理部門每半年對資
       of capital and the risks associated with each class of capital.     本結構進行一次審查。作為審查的
                                                                           一部分，管理階層會考慮資本成本
                                                                           以及各類資本相關的風險。
